class=\"css-15lb7g6-markdownText_body-content\"><p>Looking for an apartment in Stockholm, Gothenburg, Malmö, Lund or Helsingborg? Explore flexible accommodation designed for businesses – a practical solution for employees, project-based staff and consultants. With corporate apartments, arranging a temporary stay becomes straightforward, while still offering the comfort and sense of home that longer assignments often require.</p></div></div></div></section><x-scroll-anchor id=\"foeretagsbostaeder\" role=\"none\"></x-scroll-anchor><section class=\"css-jdx7f0-text-media-card\"><div class=\"wrapper css-108mid2\"><img src=\"https://images.ctfassets.net/nwbqij9m1jag/7dECdZbW86OTgJIwFSK967/dda8ac832e72a91a9ef06b8081936a5c/Namnl%C3%B6s_design_1_1?fm=webp&amp;q=80&amp;w=1024\" alt=\"A person taking a selfie with a smartphone in front of a tall building and a small tree.\" loading=\"lazy\" class=\"css-4x1fyr\" width=\"908\" height=\"908\"><div class=\"css-zenzvr\"><p class=\"css-oeeqlz\">Housing solutions for businesses</p><h2 class=\"css-1i7qdus\">Corporate housing</h2><div class=\"css-2iot6y\"><p>For decades, Företagsbostäder has supported companies in securing practical accommodation for temporary stays and is now a trusted housing partner to many of Sweden’s leading businesses. The setup allows residents to settle into their own routines and feel at home from day one.</p></div><div class=\"css-afldaz\"><a href=\"https://www.foretagsbostader.se/en\" target=\"_self\" arialabel=\"Explore available apartments\" aria-label=\"Explore available apartments\" class=\"css-puh3lk\"><span>Explore available apartments</span></a></div></div></div></section><x-scroll-anchor id=\"vanliga-fragor-och-svar\" role=\"none\"></x-scroll-anchor><section class=\"css-ey72m7-text-media-card\"><div class=\"wrapper css-108mid2\"><img src=\"https://images.ctfassets.net/nwbqij9m1jag/30zWTpTOtUbhvybpeh3LqU/93c21e24752f2a2b548e62561c0ecc87/Namnl%C3%B6s_design__1__1_1?fm=webp&amp;q=80&amp;w=1024\" alt=\"A person kneeling and gently tending to a potted plant with large green leaves.\" loading=\"lazy\" class=\"css-14hq8t5\" width=\"908\" height=\"908\"><div class=\"css-zenzvr\"><p class=\"css-oeeqlz\">Fully equipped accommodation</p><h2 class=\"css-1i7qdus\">What’s included in the apartment</h2><div class=\"css-2iot6y\"><p>All corporate apartments are fully furnished and equipped to ensure a comfortable stay.
